"DOTA_Tooltip_ability_Primary_morph_Description"	"Change your own form and transform the secondary attribute into the primary attribute. This process is reversible. Under automatic casting, to convert the main attribute to the secondary attribute. Passively obtain the primary attribute bonus."

"DOTA_Tooltip_ability_Primary_Overload"	"Primary Overload"
"DOTA_Tooltip_ability_Primary_Overload_Description"	"When casting a spell, it will generate electric charge, which will be released during the next attack, causing damage to up to 4 enemies around the target and slowing down their attack speed and move speed."
"DOTA_Tooltip_ability_Primary_Overload_Lore"	"Modern science tells us that any object is composed of atoms, and atoms are composed of positively charged atomic nuclei and negatively charged electrons, which move around the atomic nucleus. In general, the number of positive charges in the atomic nucleus is equal to the number of negative charges in the extranuclear electron band. The atom is not electrically sensitive, so the whole object is neutral. It is difficult to change the number of positive charges in the nucleus, but the electrons outside the nucleus can get rid of the nucleus and transfer to another object, thus changing the number of negative charges in the electron band outside the nucleus. When an object loses its electrons, the total negative charge of its electrons is less than the positive charge of the atomic nucleus, indicating that it is positively charged; On the contrary, a neutral object will show negative charge when it gets electrons."
